 def _ensure_scheduled_schema() -> None:
     """Allow multiple daily schedules per symbol at different trigger times."""
     if not DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite"):
         return
     try:
         with engine.begin() as conn:
             tables = {row[0] for row in conn.execute(text("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"))}
             if "scheduled_analyses" not in tables:
                 return
 
             index_rows = conn.execute(text("PRAGMA index_list(scheduled_analyses)")).fetchall()
             unique_columns: list[list[str]] = []
             for row in index_rows:
                 index_name = row[1]
                 is_unique = bool(row[2])
                 if not is_unique:
                     continue
                 cols = [info[2] for info in conn.execute(text(f"PRAGMA index_info({index_name})")).fetchall()]
                 unique_columns.append(cols)
 
             has_new_unique = ["user_id", "symbol", "trigger_time"] in unique_columns
             has_old_unique = ["user_id", "symbol"] in unique_columns
             if has_new_unique or not has_old_unique:
                 return
 
             logger.info("[migration] Rebuilding scheduled_analyses unique constraint for dual schedules.")
             conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS scheduled_analyses_new"))
             conn.execute(
                 text(
                     """
                     CREATE TABLE scheduled_analyses_new (
                         id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
                         user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
                         symbol VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
                         horizon VARCHAR(10),
                         trigger_time VARCHAR(5),
                         is_active BOOLEAN,
                         last_run_date VARCHAR(10),
                         last_run_status VARCHAR(10),
                         last_report_id VARCHAR(36),
                         consecutive_failures INTEGER,
                         created_at DATETIME,
                         updated_at DATETIME,
                         CONSTRAINT uq_scheduled_user_symbol_time UNIQUE (user_id, symbol, trigger_time)
                     )
                     """
                 )
             )
             conn.execute(
                 text(
                     """
                     INSERT INTO scheduled_analyses_new (
                         id, user_id, symbol, horizon, trigger_time, is_active,
                         last_run_date, last_run_status, last_report_id,
                         consecutive_failures, created_at, updated_at
                     )
                     SELECT
                         id, user_id, symbol, horizon, COALESCE(trigger_time, '20:00'), is_active,
                         last_run_date, last_run_status, last_report_id,
                         consecutive_failures, created_at, updated_at
                     FROM scheduled_analyses
                     """
                 )
             )
             conn.execute(text("DROP TABLE scheduled_analyses"))
             conn.execute(text("ALTER TABLE scheduled_analyses_new RENAME TO scheduled_analyses"))
             conn.execute(text("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_scheduled_analyses_user_id ON scheduled_analyses (user_id)"))
     except Exception as e:
         logger.error("Failed to ensure scheduled schema: %s", e)
 
 
+# [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] Legacy intraday checkpoint migration (14:30 → 13:15).
+# Provenance is explicit, not calendar-inferred: every 14:30 row that exists
+# the first time this migration runs against a database was created under the
+# old 14:30 default; every 14:30 row created afterwards is a deliberate user
+# choice and is never touched.
+_LEGACY_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME = "14:30"
+_UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME = "13:15"
+_SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID = "scheduled_intraday_1430_to_1315_v1"
+
+
+def _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=None) -> int:
+    """[CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] One-shot 14:30 → 13:15 intraday migration.
+
+    Runs from ``init_db()`` (API + scheduler) but executes at most once per
+    database, guarded by an explicit marker row in ``schema_migrations``.
+    Handles both legacy shapes:
+    - No 13:15 row for (user_id, symbol): rewrite 14:30 → 13:15.
+    - 13:15 row already exists (the frontend/backend mismatch window this
+      task repairs): deactivate the legacy 14:30 duplicate — its row and run
+      state are preserved in place — so the scheduler can no longer claim
+      both rows and run duplicate paid analyses for the same symbol. The
+      surviving 13:15 row is (re)activated only when the legacy duplicate was
+      active and the 13:15 row was not, so a user with a running intraday
+      task never silently loses it and a user who disabled theirs does not
+      get it re-enabled.
+    Returns the number of legacy rows handled (0 on no-op or failure).
+    """
+    try:
+        target = bind if bind is not None else engine
+        with target.begin() as conn:
+            conn.execute(
+                text(
+                    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations ("
+                    "migration_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, "
+                    "applied_at DATETIME)"
+                )
+            )
+            applied = conn.execute(
+                text(
+                    "SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE migration_id = :mid"
+                ),
+                {"mid": _SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID},
+            ).fetchone()
+            if applied:
+                return 0
+
+            legacy_rows = conn.execute(
+                text(
+                    "SELECT id, user_id, symbol, is_active FROM scheduled_analyses "
+                    "WHERE trigger_time = :legacy"
+                ),
+                {"legacy": _LEGACY_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME},
+            ).fetchall()
+
+            unified_rows = conn.execute(
+                text(
+                    "SELECT user_id, symbol, is_active FROM scheduled_analyses "
+                    "WHERE trigger_time = :unified"
+                ),
+                {"unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME},
+            ).fetchall()
+            unified_keys = {(row[0], row[1]) for row in unified_rows}
+            unified_active = {(row[0], row[1]) for row in unified_rows if row[2]}
+
+            converted = 0
+            deduped = 0
+            for row_id, user_id, symbol, legacy_active in legacy_rows:
+                key = (user_id, symbol)
+                if key not in unified_keys:
+                    conn.execute(
+                        text(
+                            "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
+                            "SET trigger_time = :unified, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
+                            "WHERE id = :row_id"
+                        ),
+                        {
+                            "unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME,
+                            "row_id": row_id,
+                        },
+                    )
+                    converted += 1
+                    continue
+                # Duplicate from the 14:30/13:15 mismatch window: keep exactly
+                # one intraday task at the unified 13:15 time and deactivate
+                # the legacy duplicate (row + run state preserved in place).
+                if legacy_active and key not in unified_active:
+                    conn.execute(
+                        text(
+                            "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
+                            "SET is_active = 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
+                            "WHERE user_id = :uid AND symbol = :sym "
+                            "AND trigger_time = :unified"
+                        ),
+                        {
+                            "uid": user_id,
+                            "sym": symbol,
+                            "unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME,
+                        },
+                    )
+                    unified_active.add(key)
+                conn.execute(
+                    text(
+                        "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
+                        "SET is_active = 0, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
+                        "WHERE id = :row_id"
+                    ),
+                    {"row_id": row_id},
+                )
+                deduped += 1
+
+            conn.execute(
+                text(
+                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations (migration_id, applied_at) "
+                    "VALUES (:mid, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
+                ),
+                {"mid": _SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID},
+            )
+        if converted or deduped:
+            logger.info(
+                "[migration] 14:30→13:15 intraday schedules: %s converted, "
+                "%s duplicate(s) deactivated.",
+                converted,
+                deduped,
+            )
+        return converted + deduped
+    except Exception as e:
+        logger.error("Failed to migrate scheduled intraday trigger time: %s", e)
+        return 0
+

 class ReportDB(Base):
     """Report database model."""
     
     __tablename__ = "reports"
     
     id = Column(String(36), primary_key=True, index=True)
     user_id = Column(String(64), index=True, nullable=True)  # For future multi-user support
     symbol = Column(String(20), index=True, nullable=False)
     trade_date = Column(String(10), nullable=False)
     
     # Task lifecycle info
     status = Column(String(20), default="completed", index=True)  # pending, running, completed, failed
     error = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     
     # Decision info
     decision = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # BUY, SELL, HOLD, etc.
     direction = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # 看多、偏多、中性、偏空、看空
     research_direction = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # 看多、偏多、中性、偏空、看空
     execution_action = Column(String(20), nullable=True)  # WAIT, ENTER, HOLD, REDUCE, EXIT
     action_label = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # 等待触发、条件入场、持有、回避等
     confidence = Column(Integer, nullable=True)  # 0-100
     target_price = Column(Float, nullable=True)
     stop_loss_price = Column(Float, nullable=True)
     
     # Full analysis results stored as JSON
     result_data = Column(JSON, nullable=True)
 
     # LLM-extracted structured data
     risk_items = Column(JSON, nullable=True)   # [{"name": "...", "level": "high|medium|low", "description": "..."}]
     key_metrics = Column(JSON, nullable=True)  # [{"name": "...", "value": "...", "status": "good|neutral|bad"}]
     analyst_traces = Column(JSON, nullable=True) # [{"agent": "...", "verdict": "...", "key_finding": "..."}]
 
     # Individual reports (for quick access)
     market_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     sentiment_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     news_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     fundamentals_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     macro_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     smart_money_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     volume_price_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     game_theory_report = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     investment_plan = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     trader_investment_plan = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     final_trade_decision = Column(Text, nullable=True)
     
     # Metadata
     created_at = Column(DateTime, default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
     updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc), onupdate=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
     
     def to_dict(self) -> dict:
         """Convert to dictionary."""
         return {
             "id": self.id,
             "user_id": self.user_id,
             "symbol": self.symbol,
             "trade_date": self.trade_date,
             "decision": self.decision,
             "direction": self.direction,
             "research_direction": self.research_direction,
             "execution_action": self.execution_action,
             "action_label": self.action_label,
             "confidence": self.confidence,
             "target_price": self.target_price,
             "stop_loss_price": self.stop_loss_price,
             "result_data": self.result_data,
             "risk_items": self.risk_items,
             "key_metrics": self.key_metrics,
             "analyst_traces": self.analyst_traces,
             "market_report": self.market_report,
             "sentiment_report": self.sentiment_report,
             "news_report": self.news_report,
             "fundamentals_report": self.fundamentals_report,
             "macro_report": self.macro_report,
             "smart_money_report": self.smart_money_report,
             "volume_price_report": self.volume_price_report,
             "game_theory_report": self.game_theory_report,
             "investment_plan": self.investment_plan,
             "trader_investment_plan": self.trader_investment_plan,
             "final_trade_decision": self.final_trade_decision,

+class SchemaMigrationDB(Base):
+    """[CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] Applied one-shot data migration markers.

+    """
+    __tablename__ = "schema_migrations"

+    applied_at = Column(DateTime, default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))

420-#### FUND-003A-B 补修验收口径
421-
422-- 证据的 `direction`/`negation`/因果关系必须属于具体术语及其出现位置，禁止挂在整条 entry 上被其他指标复用。
423-- 同一指标多次出现时不得只读第一次；存在相反方向或语义不明时 fail closed。
424-- 因果同义词必须同时应用于证据侧和 claim 侧；`采购成本下降` 可支持 `原材料成本下降`，但不得只因同时出现“原材料”就证明任意因果。
425-- `claim_id` 必须确定性且能区分同一报告内同指标的多条声明，不得只对 keyword 做 hash。
426-- 每个 `evidence_id` 的审计片段必须包含它所支持的具体术语、方向与否定信息。
427-- 必测对抗样例：中性首次出现+后文上涨；`未采用净额法，仍采用总额法`；`原材料下降+合同负债增加`；同指标多条相反声明；多术语长证据片段。
428-- 完成标准：专项与相关回归全绿，`codex review --uncommitted` 无 P0/P1/P2 correctness finding；不调 live LLM，不写生产数据库。
429-
430-
431-### F-001: TA研报执行层修复（P0-P2共10项）
432-- **描述**：修复002837英维克报告暴露的10个问题，涉及止损价解析、估值sanity check、Opportunity Score封顶、未持仓语义、Buy/Risk Level冲突、舆情VERDICT矛盾、入场区间误识别、打法标签、A股做空措辞、Evidence Coverage保守化
433-- **优先级**：P0

     `openclaw_callback_service.py` `_extract_system_diagnostics_offset`）：
     原实现两者都返回 None，解析会回退 legacy last-`[C-008]` 启发式，可能给
     畸形报告发布伪造 readiness。现引入 `_OFFSET_ABSENT` 哨兵区分"键缺失"
     （legacy 回退）与"键存在但值非法"（含显式 null → fail-closed 输出
     `not_available`，不回退启发式）；`offset=None 视同历史缺省回退` 的
     round2 语义被本条废止。
   - **P2 `available` 未校验字段完整性**（`build_callback_payload`）：原实现
     只要 readiness_values 非空就发 `status: available`，截断块/越界完整度/
     结构化快照字段缺失或类型错误时会带 null/invalid 字段发布，违反 v1.1
     契约。现新增 `_validated_readiness_values`：仅当 `data_completeness`
     为 0-100 整数（拒 bool/float/str）且 `confidence` ∈ {高,中,低} 时才发布
     `available`，否则尝试下一来源，全部无效输出 `not_available`。
 - 修复 round3 Codex review 追加发现（档案
   `docs/reviews/B-002-R2-20260817-round3.txt`）：
   - **P2 legacy 回退接受裸 `[C-008]` 行内提及**：正文如"参考 [C-008]
     数据完整度：99% 置信度：高"在无 offset 时会被发布。现解析只认系统
     确定性结构：`[C-008] 执行就绪度评分` 标题（`_C008_HEADING_PATTERN`）+
     行锚定字段行（`^-[ \t]*数据完整度…%$` / `^-[ \t]*置信度…$`，与
     `readiness_score.py:format_readiness_score` 的输出格式一致），行内
     prose 提及一律拒绝。
   - **P3 测试证据过期**：归档与 DEVLOG 仍称 63 项。现新增

# [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] tests
"""Tests for CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1.

Covers:
1. P1 — DeepSeek explicit authorization gate: default blocked, explicit
   switch to lift, fail-closed when the switch is off — enforced by the
   gate on the dispatcher itself, including direct construction.
2. P2 — 13:15 default intraday time: backend defaults unified with the
   frontend Portfolio.tsx DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TASKS contract.
3. P2 — Legacy 14:30 → 13:15 scheduled-task migration: one-shot via an
   explicit schema_migrations marker (no calendar-date inference),
   deactivates 13:15/14:30 duplicates while preserving state, and prevents
   duplicate task creation on automatic position import.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from api.database import (
    Base,
    ScheduledAnalysisDB,
    SchemaMigrationDB,
    _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time,
    _SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID,
)
from api.services import scheduled_service
from tradingagents.tradeflow.gated_deep_ta import (
    DeepTADispatcher,
    check_deep_ta_gate,
)
from tradingagents.tradeflow.strategy_config import (
    DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG,
    StrategyConfig,
)


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


# ── P1: DeepSeek explicit authorization gate ──


class TestDeepSeekExplicitAuthorizationConfig:
    def test_default_config_blocks_deepseek_and_is_unauthorized(self):
        cfg = StrategyConfig()
        assert cfg.deep_ta_blocked_models == ("deepseek",)
        assert cfg.deep_ta_deepseek_authorized is False
        assert DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG.deep_ta_blocked_models == ("deepseek",)
        assert DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG.deep_ta_deepseek_authorized is False

    def test_dispatcher_class_default_blocks_deepseek(self):
        d = DeepTADispatcher()
        assert d.blocked_models == ("deepseek",)

    def test_from_config_default_keeps_deepseek_blocked(self):
        d = DeepTADispatcher.from_config()
        assert "deepseek" in d.blocked_models

    def test_from_config_unauthorized_readds_deepseek_fail_closed(self):
        # A config that drops deepseek from the blocked list WITHOUT the
        # explicit authorization switch must not silently unblock it.
        cfg = StrategyConfig(deep_ta_blocked_models=())
        d = DeepTADispatcher.from_config(cfg)
        assert "deepseek" in d.blocked_models

    def test_from_config_authorized_removes_only_deepseek(self):
        cfg = StrategyConfig(
            deep_ta_blocked_models=("deepseek", "doubao"),
            deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=True,
        )
        d = DeepTADispatcher.from_config(cfg)
        assert d.blocked_models == ("doubao",)

    def test_from_config_authorized_does_not_mutate_declared_config(self):
        cfg = StrategyConfig(
            deep_ta_blocked_models=("deepseek",),
            deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=True,
        )
        d = DeepTADispatcher.from_config(cfg)
        assert d.blocked_models == ()
        assert cfg.deep_ta_blocked_models == ("deepseek",)

    def test_to_dict_includes_authorization_switch(self):
        d = StrategyConfig().to_dict()
        assert d["deep_ta_deepseek_authorized"] is False


class TestDeepSeekGateDecisions:
    def _check(self, dispatcher: DeepTADispatcher, model: str):
        return check_deep_ta_gate(
            dispatcher=dispatcher,
            symbol="002138.SZ",
            need_deep_ta=True,
            observe_state="TRIGGERED",
            composite_score=90.0,
            completeness=0.95,
            position_context="holding",
            model=model,
        )

    def test_gate_blocks_deepseek_by_default(self):
        decision = self._check(DeepTADispatcher.from_config(), "deepseek-chat")
        assert decision.allowed is False
        assert "deepseek" in decision.reason.lower()

    def test_gate_allows_deepseek_when_authorized(self):
        cfg = StrategyConfig(deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=True)
        decision = self._check(DeepTADispatcher.from_config(cfg), "deepseek-chat")
        assert decision.allowed is True
        assert decision.model == "deepseek-chat"

    def test_gate_blocks_other_models_without_authorization_relevance(self):
        cfg = StrategyConfig(
            deep_ta_blocked_models=("deepseek", "doubao"),
            deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=True,
        )
        decision = self._check(DeepTADispatcher.from_config(cfg), "doubao-pro")
        assert decision.allowed is False

    def test_direct_construction_cannot_bypass_authorization(self):
        # [P1 round1] Authorization lives on the dispatcher and is enforced
        # by the gate itself: DeepTADispatcher(blocked_models=()) used to
        # allow deepseek-chat; it must now fail closed.
        decision = self._check(DeepTADispatcher(blocked_models=()), "deepseek-chat")
        assert decision.allowed is False
        assert "deepseek" in decision.reason.lower()

    def test_direct_construction_authorized_allows_deepseek(self):
        decision = self._check(
            DeepTADispatcher(blocked_models=(), deepseek_authorized=True),
            "deepseek-chat",
        )
        assert decision.allowed is True
        assert decision.model == "deepseek-chat"


# ── P2: 13:15 frontend/backend default unification ──


class TestDefaultTriggerTimeUnification:
    def test_backend_default_trigger_times(self):
        assert scheduled_service.DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES == ("13:15", "20:00")

    def test_frontend_default_tasks_match_backend_defaults(self):
        tsx_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "frontend" / "src" / "pages" / "Portfolio.tsx"
        assert tsx_path.exists(), "Portfolio.tsx not found"
        src = tsx_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        block = re.search(
            r"DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TASKS\s*=\s*\[(.*?)\]", src, re.DOTALL
        )
        assert block, "DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TASKS block not found in Portfolio.tsx"
        frontend_times = re.findall(r"trigger_time:\s*'(\d{2}:\d{2})'", block.group(1))

        assert frontend_times == list(
            scheduled_service.DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES
        ), (
            "frontend DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TASKS times "
            f"{frontend_times} must equal backend DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES "
            f"{list(scheduled_service.DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES)}"
        )

    def test_frontend_guidance_mentions_unified_intraday_time(self):
        tsx_path = PROJECT_ROOT / "frontend" / "src" / "pages" / "Portfolio.tsx"
        src = tsx_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "13:15" in src


# ── P2: legacy 14:30 → 13:15 migration ──


@pytest.fixture
def migration_db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    Session = sessionmaker(bind=engine)
    session = Session()
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()


def _add_task(
    db,
    user_id,
    symbol,
    trigger_time,
    created_at="2026-08-01 10:00:00",
    is_active=True,
    **extra,
):
    from uuid import uuid4

    if isinstance(created_at, str):
        created_at = datetime.fromisoformat(created_at)
    db.add(
        ScheduledAnalysisDB(
            id=uuid4().hex,
            user_id=user_id,
            symbol=symbol,
            horizon="short",
            trigger_time=trigger_time,
            is_active=is_active,
            created_at=created_at,
            **extra,
        )
    )
    db.commit()


def _states(db, user_id, symbol):
    rows = (
        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
        .filter(
            ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id,
            ScheduledAnalysisDB.symbol == symbol,
        )
        .all()
    )
    return {row.trigger_time: bool(row.is_active) for row in rows}


class TestScheduledIntradayMigration:
    def test_migrates_legacy_1430_to_1315(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-a", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        migrated = _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
            bind=migration_db.get_bind()
        )
        assert migrated == 1
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-a", "600519.SH") == {"13:15": True}

    def test_marker_row_records_migration(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-a2", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=migration_db.get_bind())
        marker = (
            migration_db.query(SchemaMigrationDB)
            .filter_by(migration_id=_SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID)
            .one_or_none()
        )
        assert marker is not None
        assert marker.applied_at is not None

    def test_marker_applied_even_with_no_legacy_rows(self, migration_db):
        # A database with no 14:30 rows still records the marker, so late
        # rows created by an old process can never be misclassified later.
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-a3", "600519.SH", "20:00")
        assert (
            _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
                bind=migration_db.get_bind()
            )
            == 0
        )
        assert migration_db.query(SchemaMigrationDB).count() == 1

    def test_dedup_deactivates_legacy_1430_when_1315_exists(self, migration_db):
        # The frontend/backend mismatch window this task repairs: the user
        # has both intraday rows. Keep exactly one active 13:15 task and
        # deactivate (not delete) the legacy 14:30 duplicate so the
        # scheduler cannot claim both rows for duplicate paid analyses.
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-b", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-b", "600519.SH", "13:15")
        migrated = _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
            bind=migration_db.get_bind()
        )
        assert migrated == 1
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-b", "600519.SH") == {
            "13:15": True,
            "14:30": False,
        }

    def test_dedup_preserves_single_active_intraday_task(self, migration_db):
        # 13:15 disabled + legacy 14:30 active: the surviving 13:15 row is
        # re-activated so a user with a running intraday task keeps one.
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-b2", "600519.SH", "14:30", is_active=True)
        _add_task(
            migration_db, "user-b2", "600519.SH", "13:15", is_active=False
        )
        _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=migration_db.get_bind())
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-b2", "600519.SH") == {
            "13:15": True,
            "14:30": False,
        }

    def test_dedup_does_not_reenable_disabled_intraday_task(self, migration_db):
        # Both rows disabled → stays disabled (no silent re-enable).
        _add_task(
            migration_db, "user-b3", "600519.SH", "14:30", is_active=False
        )
        _add_task(
            migration_db, "user-b3", "600519.SH", "13:15", is_active=False
        )
        _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=migration_db.get_bind())
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-b3", "600519.SH") == {
            "13:15": False,
            "14:30": False,
        }

    def test_dedup_preserves_legacy_row_run_state(self, migration_db):
        # Deactivation keeps the legacy row and its run state for audit.
        _add_task(
            migration_db,
            "user-b4",
            "600519.SH",
            "14:30",
            last_report_id="rep-1",
            last_run_date="2026-08-18",
            last_run_status="success",
        )
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-b4", "600519.SH", "13:15")
        _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=migration_db.get_bind())
        legacy = (
            migration_db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
            .filter_by(user_id="user-b4", trigger_time="14:30")
            .one()
        )
        assert legacy.is_active is False
        assert legacy.last_report_id == "rep-1"
        assert legacy.last_run_date == "2026-08-18"
        assert legacy.last_run_status == "success"

    def test_idempotent_on_second_run(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-c", "000001.SZ", "14:30")
        bind = migration_db.get_bind()
        assert _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=bind) == 1
        assert _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=bind) == 0
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-c", "000001.SZ") == {"13:15": True}

    def test_rows_created_after_migration_ran_are_untouched(self, migration_db):
        # Provenance is the migration marker, not created_at: a 14:30 task
        # created after the one-shot migration already ran is a deliberate
        # user choice and must stay active.
        bind = migration_db.get_bind()
        assert _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=bind) == 0
        _add_task(
            migration_db,
            "user-d",
            "300750.SZ",
            "14:30",
            created_at="2027-01-01 10:00:00",
        )
        assert _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=bind) == 0
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-d", "300750.SZ") == {"14:30": True}

    def test_legacy_status_independent_of_created_at(self, migration_db):
        # First run against a database: every existing 14:30 row was created
        # under the old 14:30 default regardless of its created_at value
        # (future timestamps included — the ORM auto-fills the current
        # timestamp on insert, which broke the old date-based inference).
        _add_task(
            migration_db,
            "user-f1",
            "600519.SH",
            "14:30",
            created_at="2027-06-01 10:00:00",
        )
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-f2", "600519.SH", "14:30", created_at=None)
        assert (
            _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
                bind=migration_db.get_bind()
            )
            == 2
        )
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-f1", "600519.SH") == {"13:15": True}
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-f2", "600519.SH") == {"13:15": True}

    def test_other_trigger_times_untouched(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-e", "600519.SH", "20:00")
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-e", "600519.SH", "11:35")
        migrated = _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
            bind=migration_db.get_bind()
        )
        assert migrated == 0
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-e", "600519.SH") == {
            "11:35": True,
            "20:00": True,
        }

    def test_migration_isolated_per_user_and_symbol(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-g1", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-g2", "600519.SH", "13:15")
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-g2", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        migrated = _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(
            bind=migration_db.get_bind()
        )
        assert migrated == 2  # one converted + one deduped
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-g1", "600519.SH") == {"13:15": True}
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-g2", "600519.SH") == {
            "13:15": True,
            "14:30": False,
        }

    def test_migration_refreshes_updated_at(self, migration_db):
        _add_task(
            migration_db,
            "user-h",
            "600519.SH",
            "14:30",
            created_at=datetime(2026, 8, 1, 9, 0, 0),
        )
        _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=migration_db.get_bind())
        row = migration_db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(
            ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == "user-h"
        ).one()
        assert row.updated_at is not None
        assert row.updated_at > datetime(2026, 8, 1, 9, 0, 0)


class TestNoDuplicateTasksAfterMigration:
    def test_auto_import_after_migration_does_not_duplicate(
        self, migration_db
    ):
        # User auto-imported positions under the old 14:30 default.

 succeeded in 0ms:

 # [M-006] gated_deep_ta_dispatch
 """Gated Deep TA Dispatch — decides whether to invoke deep TA analysis
 after an intraday observe trigger.
 
 Responsibilities:
 1. Check executable conditions before dispatching TA.
 2. Enforce daily hard cap on TA dispatch count.
-3. Block forbidden models (e.g. DeepSeek) by default.
+3. Block forbidden models (e.g. DeepSeek) by default; DeepSeek requires the
+   explicit authorization switch ``deep_ta_deepseek_authorized`` (see
+   [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] in strategy_config).
 4. Record dispatch reason, model, duration, report path.
 5. Limit retries on failure — no infinite retry loops.
 6. Require position_context for all dispatches.
 
 Design constraints:
-- Default: do NOT call DeepSeek or any blocked model.
+- Default: do NOT call DeepSeek or any blocked model. DeepSeek is only
+  unblocked when the config explicitly sets deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=True.
 - Daily TA deep analysis count has a hard upper limit.
 - need_deep_ta=False → never dispatch.
 - Exceeds daily limit → never dispatch.
 - Failures are recorded but not retried beyond max_retries.
 - Does NOT actually call any LLM — only produces a dispatch decision
   and audit trail.  The caller (OpenClaw / scheduler) is responsible
   for executing the TA if allowed.
 """
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
 from dataclasses import dataclass, field
 from datetime import datetime
 from enum import Enum
 from typing import Optional
 
 from .strategy_config import StrategyConfig, DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG  # [M-004]
 
 
 class DeepTAStatus(str, Enum):
     PENDING = "PENDING"
     BLOCKED = "BLOCKED"
     DISPATCHED = "DISPATCHED"
     SUCCESS = "SUCCESS"
     FAILED = "FAILED"
 
 
 @dataclass
 class DeepTARecord:
     symbol: str
     status: DeepTAStatus = DeepTAStatus.PENDING
     dispatch_reason: str = ""
     block_reason: str = ""
     model: str = ""
     dispatch_time: str = ""
     duration_sec: float = 0.0
     report_path: str = ""
     retry_count: int = 0
     position_context: str = ""
     composite_score: float = 0.0
     observe_state: str = ""
     completeness: float = 0.0
     trade_date: str = ""
 
     def __post_init__(self):
         if not self.trade_date:
             self.trade_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
 
 
 @dataclass
 class DeepTADecision:
     allowed: bool = False
     reason: str = ""
     model: str = ""
     record: Optional[DeepTARecord] = None
 
 
 @dataclass
 class DeepTADispatcher:
     trade_date: str = ""
     daily_count: int = 0
     daily_limit: int = 3
-    blocked_models: tuple = ()  # 2026-08-15: 解除 deepseek 黑名单（V4 GA 后恢复使用）
+    blocked_models: tuple = ("deepseek",)  # [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] default block restored; explicit authorization required to lift
+    # [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] DeepSeek explicit authorization is carried on the
+    # dispatcher itself and enforced by check_deep_ta_gate, so direct
+    # construction (not just from_config) cannot bypass the paid-model gate.
+    deepseek_authorized: bool = False
     default_model: str = ""
     max_retries: int = 1
     min_composite_score: float = 40.0
     min_completeness: float = 0.5
     require_observe_triggered: bool = True
     records: dict[str, DeepTARecord] = field(default_factory=dict)
     failure_counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
 
     def __post_init__(self):
         if not self.trade_date:
             self.trade_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
 
     @classmethod
     def from_config(cls, cfg: Optional[StrategyConfig] = None) -> "DeepTADispatcher":
         if cfg is None:
             cfg = DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG
+        deepseek_authorized = bool(getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_deepseek_authorized", False))
+        blocked_models = tuple(getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_blocked_models", ("deepseek",)))
+        # [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] DeepSeek explicit authorization gate:
+        # - unauthorized (default): keep "deepseek" blocked even if a config
+        #   accidentally dropped it — fail closed, never implicitly unblocked.
+        # - authorized: drop only "deepseek"; every other blocked entry stays.
+        if deepseek_authorized:
+            blocked_models = tuple(
+                m for m in blocked_models if str(m).lower() != "deepseek"
+            )
+        elif "deepseek" not in {str(m).lower() for m in blocked_models}:
+            blocked_models = blocked_models + ("deepseek",)
         return cls(
             daily_limit=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_daily_limit", 3),
-            blocked_models=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_blocked_models", ("deepseek",)),
+            blocked_models=blocked_models,
+            deepseek_authorized=deepseek_authorized,
             default_model=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_default_model", ""),
             max_retries=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_max_retries", 1),
             min_composite_score=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_min_composite_score", 40.0),
             min_completeness=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_min_completeness", 0.5),
             require_observe_triggered=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_require_observe_triggered", True),
         )
 
 
 def check_deep_ta_gate(
     dispatcher: DeepTADispatcher,
     symbol: str,
     need_deep_ta: bool,
     observe_state: str,
     composite_score: float = 0.0,
     completeness: float = 0.0,
     position_context: str = "unknown",
     tier: str = "",
     model: str = "",
     retry_count: int = 0,
 ) -> DeepTADecision:
     """Check all gates before allowing a deep TA dispatch.
 
     Returns a DeepTADecision with allowed=True only if all gates pass.
     """
     record = DeepTARecord(
         symbol=symbol,
         position_context=position_context,
         composite_score=composite_score,
         observe_state=observe_state,
         completeness=completeness,
         trade_date=dispatcher.trade_date,
     )
 
     if not need_deep_ta:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = "need_deep_ta=False"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason="need_deep_ta=False", record=record)
 
     if dispatcher.daily_count >= dispatcher.daily_limit:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = f"每日次数上限({dispatcher.daily_limit}次)已满"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     if dispatcher.require_observe_triggered and observe_state != "TRIGGERED":
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = f"盘中观察状态非TRIGGERED(当前:{observe_state})"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     if composite_score < dispatcher.min_composite_score:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = f"综合分{composite_score:.1f}低于阈值{dispatcher.min_composite_score:.1f}"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     if completeness < dispatcher.min_completeness:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = f"证据完整度{completeness:.0%}低于阈值{dispatcher.min_completeness:.0%}"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     resolved_model = model or dispatcher.default_model
     if not resolved_model:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = "未指定模型(default_model也为空)"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     model_lower = resolved_model.lower()
+    # [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] DeepSeek requires explicit authorization carried
+    # on the dispatcher itself. Enforced here in the gate — not only in
+    # from_config — so direct construction with a cleared blocked_models tuple
+    # still cannot bypass the paid-model protection.
+    if "deepseek" in model_lower and not dispatcher.deepseek_authorized:
+        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
+        record.block_reason = (
+            f"模型'{resolved_model}'未获DeepSeek显式授权"
+            "(deep_ta_deepseek_authorized=False)"
+        )
+        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
     for blocked in dispatcher.blocked_models:
         if blocked and blocked.lower() in model_lower:
             record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
             record.block_reason = f"模型'{resolved_model}'被默认屏蔽"
             return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     sym_failures = dispatcher.failure_counts.get(symbol, 0)
     if sym_failures + retry_count > dispatcher.max_retries:
         record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
         record.block_reason = f"失败次数({sym_failures + retry_count})超过最大重试({dispatcher.max_retries})"
         return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)
 
     if not position_context or position_context == "unknown":
         record.position_context = "unknown"
 
     record.status = DeepTAStatus.PENDING
     record.model = resolved_model
     record.retry_count = retry_count
 
     reason_parts = [
         f"综合分{composite_score:.1f}",
         f"完整度{completeness:.0%}",
         f"观察状态={observe_state}",
         f"持仓={position_context}",
     ]
     if tier:
         reason_parts.append(f"分层={tier}")
     if retry_count > 0:
         reason_parts.append(f"重试#{retry_count}")
 
     return DeepTADecision(
         allowed=True,
         reason="; ".join(reason_parts),
         model=resolved_model,
         record=record,
     )
 
 
 def record_deep_ta_dispatch(
     dispatcher: DeepTADispatcher,
     symbol: str,
     status: DeepTAStatus,
     model: str = "",
     duration_sec: float = 0.0,
     report_path: str = "",
     dispatch_reason: str = "",
     position_context: str = "unknown",
 ) -> DeepTARecord:
     """Record a deep TA dispatch result and update dispatcher state.
 
     Increments daily count on DISPATCHED and failure count on FAILED.
     """
     now_iso = datetime.now().isoformat()
 
     existing = dispatcher.records.get(symbol)
     if existing is None:
         record = DeepTARecord(
             symbol=symbol,
             status=status,
             model=model,
             dispatch_time=now_iso,
             duration_sec=duration_sec,
             report_path=report_path,
             dispatch_reason=dispatch_reason,
             position_context=position_context,
             trade_date=dispatcher.trade_date,
         )
     else:
         record = existing
         record.status = status
         if model:
             record.model = model
         if duration_sec > 0:
             record.duration_sec = duration_sec
         if report_path:
             record.report_path = report_path
         if dispatch_reason:
             record.dispatch_reason = dispatch_reason
         if position_context and position_context != "unknown":
             record.position_context = position_context
         record.dispatch_time = now_iso
 
     dispatcher.records[symbol] = record
 
     if status == DeepTAStatus.DISPATCHED:
         dispatcher.daily_count += 1
     elif status == DeepTAStatus.FAILED:
         cur = dispatcher.failure_counts.get(symbol, 0)
         dispatcher.failure_counts[symbol] = cur + 1
 
     return record
 
 
 def can_retry_deep_ta(
     dispatcher: DeepTADispatcher,
     symbol: str,
     retry_count: int = 0,
 ) -> bool:
     """Check if a failed deep TA dispatch can be retried."""
     sym_failures = dispatcher.failure_counts.get(symbol, 0)
     return sym_failures + retry_count <= dispatcher.max_retries

 """Scheduled analysis service for database operations."""
 
 from typing import Iterable, List, Optional
 from uuid import uuid4

 import sqlalchemy
 
 from api.database import ScheduledAnalysisDB
 
 MAX_SCHEDULED_ITEMS = 10
-DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES = ("14:30", "20:00")

+# Portfolio.tsx DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TASKS (13:15 盘中 + 20:00 盘后).
+DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES = ("13:15", "20:00")
 
 VALID_HORIZONS = {"short", "medium"}
 
 
 def _validate_trigger_time(t: str) -> str:
     """Validate HH:MM format.
 
     The scheduler now supports both intraday watch points and post-close
-    review points. Frontend guidance can still recommend 11:35/14:30/20:00,
+    review points. Frontend guidance can still recommend 11:35/13:15/20:00,
     but the backend only enforces a valid wall-clock time.
     """
     parts = t.strip().split(":")
     if len(parts) != 2:
         raise ValueError("时间格式错误，请使用 HH:MM")
     try:
         hh, mm = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])
     except ValueError:
         raise ValueError("时间格式错误，请使用 HH:MM")
     if not (0 <= hh <= 23 and 0 <= mm <= 59):
         raise ValueError("时间格式错误，请使用 HH:MM")
     return f"{hh:02d}:{mm:02d}"
 
 
 def _ensure_no_schedule_conflict(
     db: Session,
     user_id: str,
     symbol: str,
     trigger_time: str,
     *,
     exclude_id: Optional[str] = None,
 ) -> None:
     query = db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(
         ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id,
         ScheduledAnalysisDB.symbol == symbol,
         ScheduledAnalysisDB.trigger_time == trigger_time,
     )
     if exclude_id:
         query = query.filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id != exclude_id)
     if query.first():
         raise ValueError(f"{symbol} 在 {trigger_time} 已有定时分析任务")
 
 
 def list_scheduled(db: Session, user_id: str) -> List[dict]:
     """List user's scheduled analysis tasks."""
     items = (
         db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
         .filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id)
         .order_by(ScheduledAnalysisDB.created_at)
         .all()
     )
     return [_to_dict(item) for item in items]
 
 
 def get_scheduled(db: Session, user_id: str, item_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
     """Get a single scheduled analysis task for the user."""
     item = (
         db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
         .filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id, ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id)
         .first()
     )
     if not item:
         return None
     return _to_dict(item)
 
 
 def get_scheduled_batch(db: Session, user_id: str, item_ids: Iterable[str]) -> List[dict]:
     """Get multiple scheduled analysis tasks in the requested order."""
 
     normalized_ids = _normalize_item_ids(item_ids)
     if not normalized_ids:
         raise ValueError("请至少选择一个定时任务")
 
     items = (
         db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
         .filter(
             ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id,
             ScheduledAnalysisDB.id.in_(normalized_ids),
         )
         .all()
     )
     item_map = {item.id: item for item in items}
     missing_ids = [item_id for item_id in normalized_ids if item_id not in item_map]
     if missing_ids:
         raise ValueError("部分定时任务不存在或已失效，请刷新后重试")
 
     return [_to_dict(item_map[item_id]) for item_id in normalized_ids]
 
 
 def _normalize_item_ids(item_ids: Iterable[str]) -> list[str]:
     normalized: list[str] = []
     seen: set[str] = set()
     for raw_id in item_ids:
         item_id = (raw_id or "").strip()
         if not item_id or item_id in seen:
             continue
         seen.add(item_id)
         normalized.append(item_id)
     return normalized
 
 
 def _validate_horizon(horizon: str) -> str:
     if horizon not in VALID_HORIZONS:
         raise ValueError("horizon 必须为 short 或 medium")
     return horizon
 
 
 def _apply_scheduled_updates(item: ScheduledAnalysisDB, **kwargs) -> None:
     if "is_active" in kwargs:
         item.is_active = kwargs["is_active"]

   364	# [CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] Legacy intraday checkpoint migration (14:30 → 13:15).
   365	# Provenance is explicit, not calendar-inferred: every 14:30 row that exists
   366	# the first time this migration runs against a database was created under the
   367	# old 14:30 default; every 14:30 row created afterwards is a deliberate user
   368	# choice and is never touched.
   369	_LEGACY_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME = "14:30"
   370	_UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME = "13:15"
   371	_SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID = "scheduled_intraday_1430_to_1315_v1"
   372	
   373	
   374	def _migrate_scheduled_intraday_trigger_time(bind=None) -> int:
   375	    """[CONFIG-DS-SCHEDULE-R1] One-shot 14:30 → 13:15 intraday migration.
   376	
   377	    Runs from ``init_db()`` (API + scheduler) but executes at most once per
   378	    database, guarded by an explicit marker row in ``schema_migrations``.
   379	    Handles both legacy shapes:
   380	    - No 13:15 row for (user_id, symbol): rewrite 14:30 → 13:15.
   381	    - 13:15 row already exists (the frontend/backend mismatch window this
   382	      task repairs): deactivate the legacy 14:30 duplicate — its row and run
   383	      state are preserved in place — so the scheduler can no longer claim
   384	      both rows and run duplicate paid analyses for the same symbol. The
   385	      surviving 13:15 row is (re)activated only when the legacy duplicate was
   386	      active and the 13:15 row was not, so a user with a running intraday
   387	      task never silently loses it and a user who disabled theirs does not
   388	      get it re-enabled.
   389	    Returns the number of legacy rows handled (0 on no-op or failure).
   390	    """
   391	    try:
   392	        target = bind if bind is not None else engine
   393	        with target.begin() as conn:
   394	            conn.execute(
   395	                text(
   396	                    "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schema_migrations ("
   397	                    "migration_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, "
   398	                    "applied_at DATETIME)"
   399	                )
   400	            )
   401	            applied = conn.execute(
   402	                text(
   403	                    "SELECT 1 FROM schema_migrations WHERE migration_id = :mid"
   404	                ),
   405	                {"mid": _SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID},
   406	            ).fetchone()
   407	            if applied:
   408	                return 0
   409	
   410	            legacy_rows = conn.execute(
   411	                text(
   412	                    "SELECT id, user_id, symbol, is_active FROM scheduled_analyses "
   413	                    "WHERE trigger_time = :legacy"
   414	                ),
   415	                {"legacy": _LEGACY_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME},
   416	            ).fetchall()
   417	
   418	            unified_rows = conn.execute(
   419	                text(
   420	                    "SELECT user_id, symbol, is_active FROM scheduled_analyses "
   421	                    "WHERE trigger_time = :unified"
   422	                ),
   423	                {"unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME},
   424	            ).fetchall()
   425	            unified_keys = {(row[0], row[1]) for row in unified_rows}
   426	            unified_active = {(row[0], row[1]) for row in unified_rows if row[2]}
   427	
   428	            converted = 0
   429	            deduped = 0
   430	            for row_id, user_id, symbol, legacy_active in legacy_rows:
   431	                key = (user_id, symbol)
   432	                if key not in unified_keys:
   433	                    conn.execute(
   434	                        text(
   435	                            "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
   436	                            "SET trigger_time = :unified, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
   437	                            "WHERE id = :row_id"
   438	                        ),
   439	                        {
   440	                            "unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME,
   441	                            "row_id": row_id,
   442	                        },
   443	                    )
   444	                    converted += 1
   445	                    continue
   446	                # Duplicate from the 14:30/13:15 mismatch window: keep exactly
   447	                # one intraday task at the unified 13:15 time and deactivate
   448	                # the legacy duplicate (row + run state preserved in place).
   449	                if legacy_active and key not in unified_active:
   450	                    conn.execute(
   451	                        text(
   452	                            "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
   453	                            "SET is_active = 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
   454	                            "WHERE user_id = :uid AND symbol = :sym "
   455	                            "AND trigger_time = :unified"
   456	                        ),
   457	                        {
   458	                            "uid": user_id,
   459	                            "sym": symbol,
   460	                            "unified": _UNIFIED_INTRADAY_TRIGGER_TIME,
   461	                        },
   462	                    )
   463	                    unified_active.add(key)
   464	                conn.execute(
   465	                    text(
   466	                        "UPDATE scheduled_analyses "
   467	                        "SET is_active = 0, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP "
   468	                        "WHERE id = :row_id"
   469	                    ),
   470	                    {"row_id": row_id},
   471	                )
   472	                deduped += 1
   473	
   474	            conn.execute(
   475	                text(
   476	                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations (migration_id, applied_at) "
   477	                    "VALUES (:mid, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)"
   478	                ),
   479	                {"mid": _SCHEDULED_1315_MIGRATION_ID},
   480	            )
   481	        if converted or deduped:
   482	            logger.info(
   483	                "[migration] 14:30→13:15 intraday schedules: %s converted, "
   484	                "%s duplicate(s) deactivated.",
   485	                converted,
   486	                deduped,
   487	            )
   488	        return converted + deduped
   489	    except Exception as e:
   490	        logger.error("Failed to migrate scheduled intraday trigger time: %s", e)
   491	        return 0

    def test_import_without_migration_would_have_duplicated(
        self, migration_db
    ):
        # Negative control: without migration the new default would create a
        # duplicate intraday task (this is the bug the migration closes).
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-nomigrate", "600519.SH", "14:30")
        _add_task(migration_db, "user-nomigrate", "600519.SH", "20:00")

        result = scheduled_service.ensure_scheduled_for_symbols(
            db=migration_db,
            user_id="user-nomigrate",
            symbols=["600519.SH"],
        )
        migration_db.commit()

        assert result["created"] == ["600519.SH"]
        assert _states(migration_db, "user-nomigrate", "600519.SH") == {
            "13:15": True,
            "14:30": True,
            "20:00": True,

    def __post_init__(self):

@dataclass
class DeepTADecision:
    allowed: bool = False
    reason: str = ""
    model: str = ""
    record: Optional[DeepTARecord] = None


@dataclass
class DeepTADispatcher:
    trade_date: str = ""
    daily_count: int = 0
    daily_limit: int = 3
    blocked_models: tuple = ()  # 2026-08-15: 解除 deepseek 黑名单（V4 GA 后恢复使用）
    default_model: str = ""
    max_retries: int = 1
    min_composite_score: float = 40.0
    min_completeness: float = 0.5
    require_observe_triggered: bool = True
    records: dict[str, DeepTARecord] = field(default_factory=dict)
    failure_counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.trade_date:
            self.trade_date = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

    @classmethod
    def from_config(cls, cfg: Optional[StrategyConfig] = None) -> "DeepTADispatcher":
        if cfg is None:
            cfg = DEFAULT_STRATEGY_CONFIG
        return cls(
            daily_limit=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_daily_limit", 3),
            blocked_models=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_blocked_models", ("deepseek",)),
            default_model=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_default_model", ""),
            max_retries=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_max_retries", 1),
            min_composite_score=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_min_composite_score", 40.0),
            min_completeness=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_min_completeness", 0.5),
            require_observe_triggered=getattr(cfg, "deep_ta_require_observe_triggered", True),
        )


def check_deep_ta_gate(
    dispatcher: DeepTADispatcher,
    symbol: str,
    need_deep_ta: bool,
    observe_state: str,
    composite_score: float = 0.0,
    completeness: float = 0.0,
    position_context: str = "unknown",
    tier: str = "",
    model: str = "",
    retry_count: int = 0,
) -> DeepTADecision:
    """Check all gates before allowing a deep TA dispatch.

    Returns a DeepTADecision with allowed=True only if all gates pass.
    """
    record = DeepTARecord(
        symbol=symbol,
        position_context=position_context,
        composite_score=composite_score,
        observe_state=observe_state,
        completeness=completeness,
        trade_date=dispatcher.trade_date,
    )

    if not need_deep_ta:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = "need_deep_ta=False"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason="need_deep_ta=False", record=record)

    if dispatcher.daily_count >= dispatcher.daily_limit:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = f"每日次数上限({dispatcher.daily_limit}次)已满"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    if dispatcher.require_observe_triggered and observe_state != "TRIGGERED":
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = f"盘中观察状态非TRIGGERED(当前:{observe_state})"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    if composite_score < dispatcher.min_composite_score:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = f"综合分{composite_score:.1f}低于阈值{dispatcher.min_composite_score:.1f}"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    if completeness < dispatcher.min_completeness:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = f"证据完整度{completeness:.0%}低于阈值{dispatcher.min_completeness:.0%}"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    resolved_model = model or dispatcher.default_model
    if not resolved_model:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = "未指定模型(default_model也为空)"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    model_lower = resolved_model.lower()
    for blocked in dispatcher.blocked_models:
        if blocked and blocked.lower() in model_lower:
            record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
            record.block_reason = f"模型'{resolved_model}'被默认屏蔽"
            return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    sym_failures = dispatcher.failure_counts.get(symbol, 0)
    if sym_failures + retry_count > dispatcher.max_retries:
        record.status = DeepTAStatus.BLOCKED
        record.block_reason = f"失败次数({sym_failures + retry_count})超过最大重试({dispatcher.max_retries})"
        return DeepTADecision(allowed=False, reason=record.block_reason, record=record)

    if not position_context or position_context == "unknown":
        record.position_context = "unknown"

    record.status = DeepTAStatus.PENDING
    record.model = resolved_model
    record.retry_count = retry_count


--- HEAD config ---
    # Further split main_candidates into primary (高优先级) vs secondary (次优)
    # to address "candidate pool too large / looks like catching falling knives".
    # Primary 必须同时满足: 高分 + 多维共振 + 全部确认维度 + 数据充足.
    # Secondary 候选有可读 tier_reason, 仍留在 main_candidates 不丢失.
    tier_primary_min_composite: float = 65.0
    tier_primary_min_resonance: int = 3
    tier_primary_require_volume_and_capital: bool = True
    tier_primary_haotian_min_support_dims: int = 2
    tier_secondary_data_completeness_min: float = 0.5
    tier_secondary_max_count: int = 3

    # ── Deep TA Dispatch Gate [M-006] ──
    deep_ta_daily_limit: int = 3
    deep_ta_default_model: str = ""
    deep_ta_blocked_models: tuple = ()  # 2026-08-15: 解除 deepseek 黑名单（V4 GA 后恢复使用）
    deep_ta_max_retries: int = 1
    deep_ta_min_composite_score: float = 40.0
    deep_ta_min_completeness: float = 0.5
    deep_ta_require_observe_triggered: bool = True

    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
        d = asdict(self)
        d["risk_high_severity_flags"] = list(self.risk_high_severity_flags)
        return d

   115	
   116	def _validate_horizon(horizon: str) -> str:
   117	    if horizon not in VALID_HORIZONS:
   118	        raise ValueError("horizon 必须为 short 或 medium")
   119	    return horizon
   120	
   121	
   122	def _apply_scheduled_updates(item: ScheduledAnalysisDB, **kwargs) -> None:
   123	    if "is_active" in kwargs:
   124	        item.is_active = kwargs["is_active"]
   125	        if kwargs["is_active"]:
   126	            item.consecutive_failures = 0
   127	    if "horizon" in kwargs:
   128	        item.horizon = _validate_horizon(kwargs["horizon"])
   129	    if "trigger_time" in kwargs:
   130	        item.trigger_time = _validate_trigger_time(kwargs["trigger_time"])
   131	
   132	
   133	def create_scheduled(
   134	    db: Session,
   135	    user_id: str,
   136	    symbol: str,
   137	    horizon: str = "short",
   138	    trigger_time: str = "20:00",
   139	) -> dict:
   140	    """Create a scheduled analysis task."""
   141	    count = db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(
   142	        ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id
   143	    ).count()
   144	    if count >= MAX_SCHEDULED_ITEMS:
   145	        raise ValueError(f"定时分析数量已达上限 ({MAX_SCHEDULED_ITEMS})")
   146	
   147	    horizon = _validate_horizon(horizon)
   148	    trigger_time = _validate_trigger_time(trigger_time)
   149	    _ensure_no_schedule_conflict(db, user_id, symbol, trigger_time)
   150	
   151	    item = ScheduledAnalysisDB(
   152	        id=uuid4().hex,
   153	        user_id=user_id,
   154	        symbol=symbol,
   155	        horizon=horizon,
   156	        trigger_time=trigger_time,
   157	    )
   158	    db.add(item)
   159	    db.commit()
   160	    db.refresh(item)
   161	    return _to_dict(item)
   162	
   163	
   164	def ensure_scheduled_for_symbols(
   165	    db: Session,
   166	    user_id: str,
   167	    symbols: Iterable[str],
   168	    horizon: str = "short",
   169	    trigger_time: str | Iterable[str] | None = None,
   170	) -> dict:
   171	    """Ensure the given symbols exist in scheduled tasks without duplicating existing items."""
   172	
   173	    horizon = _validate_horizon(horizon)
   174	    if trigger_time is None:
   175	        trigger_times = list(DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES)
   176	    elif isinstance(trigger_time, str):
   177	        trigger_times = [_validate_trigger_time(trigger_time)]
   178	    else:
   179	        trigger_times = [_validate_trigger_time(item) for item in trigger_time]
   180	    if not trigger_times:
   181	        trigger_times = list(DEFAULT_SCHEDULED_TRIGGER_TIMES)
   182	
   183	    existing_items = (
   184	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   185	        .filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id)
   186	        .order_by(ScheduledAnalysisDB.created_at)
   187	        .all()
   188	    )
   189	    existing_keys = {(item.symbol, item.trigger_time or "20:00") for item in existing_items}
   190	    remaining_slots = max(0, MAX_SCHEDULED_ITEMS - len(existing_items))
   191	
   192	    created: list[str] = []
   193	    existing: list[str] = []
   194	    skipped_limit: list[str] = []
   195	    seen: set[str] = set()
   196	
   197	    for raw_symbol in symbols:
   198	        symbol = (raw_symbol or "").strip().upper()
   199	        if not symbol or symbol in seen:
   200	            continue
   201	        seen.add(symbol)
   202	
   203	        for trigger_time_item in trigger_times:
   204	            key = (symbol, trigger_time_item)
   205	            if key in existing_keys:
   206	                existing.append(symbol)
   207	                continue
   208	
   209	            if remaining_slots <= 0:
   210	                skipped_limit.append(symbol)
   211	                continue
   212	
   213	            db.add(
   214	                ScheduledAnalysisDB(
   215	                    id=uuid4().hex,
   216	                    user_id=user_id,
   217	                    symbol=symbol,
   218	                    horizon=horizon,
   219	                    trigger_time=trigger_time_item,
   220	                )
   221	            )
   222	            existing_keys.add(key)
   223	            created.append(symbol)
   224	            remaining_slots -= 1
   225	
   226	    if created:
   227	        db.flush()
   228	
   229	    return {
   230	        "created": created,
   231	        "existing": existing,
   232	        "skipped_limit": skipped_limit,
   233	    }
   234	
   235	
   236	def update_scheduled(db: Session, user_id: str, item_id: str, **kwargs) -> Optional[dict]:
   237	    """Update a scheduled analysis task. Returns None if not found."""
   238	    item = (
   239	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   240	        .filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id, ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id)
   241	        .first()
   242	    )
   243	    if not item:
   244	        return None
   245	
   246	    next_trigger_time = _validate_trigger_time(kwargs["trigger_time"]) if "trigger_time" in kwargs else (item.trigger_time or "20:00")
   247	    if "trigger_time" in kwargs:
   248	        _ensure_no_schedule_conflict(db, user_id, item.symbol, next_trigger_time, exclude_id=item.id)
   249	    _apply_scheduled_updates(item, **kwargs)
   250	
   251	    db.commit()
   252	    db.refresh(item)
   253	    return _to_dict(item)
   254	
   255	
   256	def batch_update_scheduled(
   257	    db: Session,
   258	    user_id: str,
   259	    item_ids: Iterable[str],
   260	    **kwargs,
   261	) -> List[dict]:
   262	    """Update multiple scheduled analysis tasks in a single transaction."""
   263	
   264	    normalized_ids = _normalize_item_ids(item_ids)
   265	    if not normalized_ids:
   266	        raise ValueError("请至少选择一个定时任务")
   267	    if not kwargs:
   268	        raise ValueError("至少提供一个更新字段")
   269	
   270	    items = (
   271	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   272	        .filter(
   273	            ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id,
   274	            ScheduledAnalysisDB.id.in_(normalized_ids),
   275	        )
   276	        .all()
   277	    )
   278	    item_map = {item.id: item for item in items}
   279	    missing_ids = [item_id for item_id in normalized_ids if item_id not in item_map]
   280	    if missing_ids:
   281	        raise ValueError("部分定时任务不存在或已失效，请刷新后重试")
   282	
   283	    if "trigger_time" in kwargs:
   284	        next_trigger_time = _validate_trigger_time(kwargs["trigger_time"])
   285	        seen_keys: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
   286	        for item_id in normalized_ids:
   287	            item = item_map[item_id]
   288	            key = (item.symbol, next_trigger_time)
   289	            if key in seen_keys:
   290	                raise ValueError(f"{item.symbol} 在 {next_trigger_time} 已有定时分析任务")
   291	            seen_keys.add(key)
   292	            _ensure_no_schedule_conflict(db, user_id, item.symbol, next_trigger_time, exclude_id=item.id)
   293	
   294	    for item_id in normalized_ids:
   295	        _apply_scheduled_updates(item_map[item_id], **kwargs)
   296	
   297	    db.commit()
   298	    for item in items:
   299	        db.refresh(item)
   300	    return [_to_dict(item_map[item_id]) for item_id in normalized_ids]
   301	
   302	
   303	def delete_scheduled(db: Session, user_id: str, item_id: str) -> bool:
   304	    """Delete a scheduled analysis task."""
   305	    item = (
   306	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   307	        .filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id, ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id)
   308	        .first()
   309	    )
   310	    if not item:
   311	        return False
   312	    db.delete(item)
   313	    db.commit()
   314	    return True
   315	
   316	
   317	def batch_delete_scheduled(db: Session, user_id: str, item_ids: Iterable[str]) -> dict:
   318	    """Delete multiple scheduled analysis tasks."""
   319	
   320	    normalized_ids = _normalize_item_ids(item_ids)
   321	    if not normalized_ids:
   322	        raise ValueError("请至少选择一个定时任务")
   323	
   324	    items = (
   325	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   326	        .filter(
   327	            ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id == user_id,
   328	            ScheduledAnalysisDB.id.in_(normalized_ids),
   329	        )
   330	        .all()
   331	    )
   332	    item_map = {item.id: item for item in items}
   333	    deleted_ids: list[str] = []
   334	    missing_ids: list[str] = []
   335	
   336	    for item_id in normalized_ids:
   337	        item = item_map.get(item_id)
   338	        if item is None:
   339	            missing_ids.append(item_id)
   340	            continue
   341	        db.delete(item)
   342	        deleted_ids.append(item_id)
   343	
   344	    if deleted_ids:
   345	        db.commit()
   346	
   347	    return {
   348	        "deleted_ids": deleted_ids,
   349	        "missing_ids": missing_ids,
   350	    }
   351	
   352	
   353	def get_pending_tasks(db: Session, today: str, current_hhmm: str) -> List[ScheduledAnalysisDB]:
   354	    """Get all active tasks that haven't run today and whose trigger time has passed.
   355	
   356	    Only returns tasks for real (non-test) users to avoid duplicate runs
   357	    from dashboard test accounts.
   358	    """
   359	    from api.database import UserDB
   360	
   360	
   361	    # Exclude dashboard test accounts (@test.com) to prevent
   362	    # 452 test users from triggering duplicate scheduled runs.
   363	    # Users NOT in the users table (e.g. test fixtures) are kept.
   364	    test_user_ids = (
   365	        db.query(UserDB.id)
   366	        .filter(UserDB.email.like("%@test.com"))
   367	        .subquery()
   368	    )
   369	
   370	    all_active = (
   371	        db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB)
   372	        .filter(
   373	            ScheduledAnalysisDB.is_active == True,
   374	            ~ScheduledAnalysisDB.user_id.in_(sqlalchemy.select(test_user_ids)),
   375	            (ScheduledAnalysisDB.last_run_date != today) | (ScheduledAnalysisDB.last_run_date == None),
   376	        )
   377	        .all()
   378	    )
   379	    # Filter by trigger_time <= current_hhmm
   380	    return [t for t in all_active if (t.trigger_time or "20:00") <= current_hhmm]
   381	
   382	
   383	def mark_run_success(db: Session, item_id: str, trade_date: str, report_id: str):
   384	    """Mark a scheduled task as successfully run."""
   385	    item = db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id).first()
   386	    if item:
   387	        item.last_run_date = trade_date
   388	        item.last_run_status = "success"
   389	        item.last_report_id = report_id
   390	        item.consecutive_failures = 0
   391	        db.commit()
   392	
   393	
   394	def mark_run_failed(db: Session, item_id: str, trade_date: str):
   395	    """Mark a scheduled task as failed. Auto-deactivate after 3 consecutive failures."""
   396	    item = db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id).first()
   397	    if item:
   398	        item.last_run_date = trade_date
   399	        item.last_run_status = "failed"
   400	        item.consecutive_failures = (item.consecutive_failures or 0) + 1
   401	        if item.consecutive_failures >= 3:
   402	            item.is_active = False
   403	        db.commit()
   404	
   405	
   406	def record_manual_test_result(
   407	    db: Session,
   408	    item_id: str,
   409	    status: str,
   410	    report_id: Optional[str] = None,
   411	) -> None:
   412	    """Record the latest manual test result without consuming the day's schedule."""
   413	    item = db.query(ScheduledAnalysisDB).filter(ScheduledAnalysisDB.id == item_id).first()
   414	    if not item:
   415	        return
   416	    item.last_run_status = status
   417	    if report_id:
   418	        item.last_report_id = report_id
   419	    if status == "success":
   420	        item.consecutive_failures = 0
   421	    db.commit()
   422	
   423	
   424	def _to_dict(item: ScheduledAnalysisDB) -> dict:
   425	    return {
   426	        "id": item.id,
   427	        "symbol": item.symbol,
   428	        "horizon": item.horizon or "short",
   429	        "trigger_time": item.trigger_time or "20:00",
   430	        "is_active": item.is_active,
   431	        "last_run_date": item.last_run_date,
   432	        "last_run_status": item.last_run_status,
   433	        "last_report_id": item.last_report_id,
   434	        "consecutive_failures": item.consecutive_failures,
   435	        "created_at": item.created_at.isoformat() if item.created_at else None,
   436	    }

2026-08-19T01:11:38.794210Z ERROR codex_models_manager::manager: failed to renew cache TTL: missing field `base_instructions` at line 95 column 5
codex
The authorization gate remains bypassable, and the schedule migration can both misclassify rows and preserve duplicate executions. The focused review run also currently fails one of the newly added migration tests.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Do not infer default schedules from a fixed date — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/api/database.py:367-370
  When rollout occurs on or after 2026-08-19, legacy 14:30 defaults created by the still-running old version after this cutoff are skipped forever, while manually chosen 14:30 schedules created before it are silently rewritten. `created_at` does not identify whether a schedule was auto-created; the focused suite already fails `test_null_created_at_rows_count_as_legacy` because the ORM supplies the current timestamp. Use explicit provenance or a migration version instead of a fixed calendar date.

- [P1] Resolve 13:15/14:30 duplicates during migration — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/api/database.py:421-424
  When a user already has both times from the frontend/backend mismatch—the exact scenario being repaired—this condition deliberately retains the legacy 14:30 row. The scheduler claims each row independently, so it runs the same symbol at 13:15 and again at 14:30, consuming another schedule slot and potentially triggering duplicate paid analyses. The migration should merge or deactivate the legacy duplicate while preserving relevant state.

- [P1] Enforce authorization on direct dispatcher construction — /Users/maybee/TradingAgents-AShare/tradingagents/tradeflow/gated_deep_ta.py:80-80
  Any caller can still construct `DeepTADispatcher(blocked_models=())` and pass `deepseek-chat`; the existing `test_empty_blocked_models_tuple` confirms that this is allowed without setting the new authorization switch. Because authorization is checked only by `from_config` and is not carried into the dispatcher or gate, this supported construction path bypasses the paid-model protection the patch claims to enforce.
